Today, a dance party is being held at the castle. Delighted, Hikaru, Umi, and Fuu set out for the castle. However, only one of them may attend the party. A great commotion erupts as they argue over who should go. At last, they decide to settle it using <02>that method<03>...[FF E0]And so the dance party draws to a close.
